Simpler: Since weights length 2n-1, a length n, compute g via faster method. Use convolution of a_rev and W. But W changes each iteration; we can compute g directly using FFT of W and A as above. Let's implement robust:
```
def grad_convolution_weights(a, w):
    n=len(a); L=len(w) # 2n-1
    size = next_pow2(L+n-1) # L+n-1 = 3n-2
    A=fft(a[::-1], size?) Wait convolution a_rev (len n) and w (len L) -> use size L+n-1.
    Ar = a[::-1]
    Arf=fft(Ar,size); Wf=fft(w,size)
    conv=np.real(ifft(Arf*Wf))
    g=conv[n-1:2*n-1] # length n
    return 2*g
```

L-BFGS with softmax parameterization:
```
def softmax_a(v):
    v = v - np.max(v) (or mean)
    e=np.exp(v)
    return e/e.sum()
def func(v):
    a=softmax_a(v)
    c=conv(a)
    # f = (maxc + logsumexp(β(c-maxc)))/β
    g_a = gradient_weighted(a,c,β)  # h = weighted ∂c? Need factor?
    # ∂f/∂a = h (since f=.../β)

def grad_topk(a, w_indices, w_vals):
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  for idx,val in zip(indices,vals):
     k=idx
     lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
     # i from lo to hi, a[k-i]
     segment = a[lo:hi+1]
     # values a[k-i] for i=lo..hi: indices k-lo down to k-hi
     rev = a[max(0,k-hi):k-lo+1]
     rev = rev[::-1]
     grad[lo:hi+1] += 2*val*rev
  return grad
```

```
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a); size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=rfft(a,size); return np.maximum(irfft(A*A, size)[:2*n-1], 0.0)
def grad_weights_dense(a,w):
    n=len(a); size=next_pow2(3*n-2)  # L 2n-1 + n -1
    W=rfft(w,size); Ar=rfft(a[::-1], size)
    conv=irfft(W*Ar,size)
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Let's reason about gradient direct function correctness and performance. We'll often use top_k sparse. Implement:
```
def grad_topk_direct(a, indices, values):
    n=a.size
    g=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k,val in zip(indices, values):
        intK=int(k)
        lo = max(0, intK - (n-1))
        hi = min(n-1, intK)
        if lo>hi: continue
        start = max(0, intK - hi)
        end = min(n-1, intK - lo) + 1
        seg = a[start:end][::-1]
        g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0 * val * seg
    return g
```
Test k=0: lo=0,hi=0,start=0,end=1, seg [a0], g0 2val a0.
For k=2n-2: lo=n-1,hi=n-1,start=n-1,end=n, seg a[n-1]. good.

Weights topk:
```
def topk_weights(c, k, beta):
   L=len(c); k=min(k,L)
   part=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   maxc=c[part].max()
   # To avoid overflow: exp(beta*(c-maxc))
   e=np.exp(beta*(c[part]-maxc))
   if beta==0: e=np.ones(k)
   s=e.sum(); if s<... e uniform
   vals=e/s
   return part, vals
```
For topk=1, vals=[1]. For topk large, maybe use beta=1 or 0. Uniform over topk maybe `beta=0`. We'll set beta parameter.

def run_pg(a_init, time_budget, rng, ..., log_prefix=''):
    n=len(a_init); a=normalize(a_init,n,1)
    x=a.copy(); best=a.copy(); best_c=max_conv_no_c(x); c_best=...

    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        # schedule
        if k < kmax: k = min(kmax, k + (1 if it % k_step ==0 else 0))
        beta = min(beta_max, beta * 1.002)
        # z
        if it > 1 and momentum >0:
            z = x + momentum*prev_delta
            # if z has sum not 1 or negative, project
            z = project_simplex(z,1.0)
        else:
            z = x
        c, curz = conv_self(z), c.max()
        if curz < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c=curz; best=z.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
        if curz < best_c? already.

        grad = grad_topk_direct(z, idx, vals)
        gmax = np.max(np.abs(grad)); if gmax<=...: break

        scale = np.max(z[z>0]) if any else 1.0/n
        alpha = theta * scale
        accepted=False
        # line search factors
        # maybe if alpha too small, increase target based on n
        chosen_x=None; chosen_c=curz; chosen_factor=0

            # optional: if step < 1e-10 break

                chosen_x=x_try; chosen_c=cur_try; chosen_factor=fac; accepted=True; break

Pseudo code with time:
```
def run_projected_gradient(a_init, budget, rng, name='', ...):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget
    n=len(a_init)
    x=normalize(a_init,n,1.0)
    c, cur = conv_self(x), c.max()
    best=x.copy(); best_c=cur
    vel=np.zeros(n)
    theta=theta0
    beta=beta0; k=k0
    it=0; no_improve=0
    last_print=start
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if it % k_rate ==0 and k<k1: k +=1
       beta=min(beta1,beta*1.001)
       # schedules: maybe final reduce k? no
       idx,vals=topk_weights(c,k,beta)
       grad=grad_topk_direct(x,idx,vals)
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(grad))
       if gmax < 1e-16: break
       d = -grad / gmax
       pos = x[x>0]
       scale = np.max(pos) if pos.size else 1.0/n
       if scale < 1e-12/n: scale=1.0/n
       step_base = theta * scale
       chosen=None; chosen_c=cur
       # If theta too small, maybe increase base
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
           step=step_base*fac
           if step < 1e-14: break
           candidate = project_simplex(x + momentum*vel - step*d, 1.0)
           c_trial, cur_trial = conv_self(candidate)
           if cur_trial < cur - 1e-17:
               chosen=candidate; chosen_c=cur_trial
               # could break, but maybe evaluate smaller factor? Larger factor accepted likely better; break
               break
       if chosen is not None:
           # update objective
           vel = 0.8*vel + 0.2*(chosen - x)  # maybe avoid large; but if chosen improves, step already good.
           # To keep momentum bounded: vel = chosen-x; simple.
           vel = chosen - x
           x = chosen; cur = chosen_c; c = c_trial
           if cur < best_c:
               best_c=cur; best=x.copy(); no_improve=0
           no_improve=0
           theta = min(theta_max, theta*(1.05 if fac==1.0 else 1.02))
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           theta = max(1e-5, theta*0.5)
           vel = np.zeros(n)
           # if no_improve > 800: perturb
           if no_improve > 800:
               # restore best and add smooth noise
               noise_scale=max(1e-5/sqrt(n), 0.005*scale)
               # smooth noise: convolve random with small kernel
               noise = rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n)
               # maybe project
               x = project_simplex(best + noise, 1.0)
               c,cur=conv_self(x)
               if cur < best_c: update best?
               vel=zeros; no_improve=0
               theta=theta0
   return best,best_c
```

Now, maybe implement a very simple but powerful "annealed multiplicative weights" for diverse starts:
```
def run_mirror(a,budget,rng,k=4,eta0=0.1):
  x=normalize
  while time:
    c,cur=conv
    idx,vals=topk_weights(c,k,1)
    grad=grad_topk_direct(x,idx,vals)
    # normalize grad max
    gm=max(abs(grad)); if gm>0:
       x *= np.exp(-eta*grad/gm)
       x = max(x,0); normalize
```
Mirror descent preserves positivity and no sort. It can make large relative changes. Could be useful for initial shape. Use eta small 0.05/0.2. We can include but time. It can generate new local minima. But final require precision, use PG.

```
x=np.arange(n)
eps=0.5
base=(x+eps)**(-alpha) * (n - x - eps)**(-alpha)
mods: constant; cos(2π f x / n) with f=1,2,3 coefficient 0.2.
a=np.maximum(base*(1+mod),0); normalize
```

def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
  old_n=len(a); x_old=np.arange(old_n); x_new=np.linspace(0,old_n-1,n_new)
  return normalize(np.interp(x_new,x_old,a),n_new)
```

Let's include a `paired mass redistribution` heuristic for endgame:
At current best, find max lag p. Among positions i where a_i a_{p-i} high, redistribute mass from high of pair to lower while preserving sum? For each pair (i,j) with i+j=p, moving δ: a_i' = a_i - t, a_j' = a_j + t (or vice versa) changes c_p by -2t(a_i + a_j?) wait product a_i a_j -> (a_i-t)(a_j+t)=a_i a_j +t(a_i-a_j)-t^2; derivative wrt t = a_i - a_j. To reduce product, transfer from larger to smaller (if both positive), making them equal (for fixed sum). But affects other lags. We can find pairs at top lags where imbalance large, do a small transfer, line search on max. This is a second-order-like coordinate operation that can reduce quadratic peaks. For nonconvex, equalizing pair sum at a peak reduces that c directly without changing sum. Great! For a single peak p, optimal product for fixed sum of a_i+a_j is when equal. But moving mass changes c for other lags. We can greedily transfer from larger to smaller for pairs of active peak lags where new max improves. This could be very effective and doesn't require full gradient.

Algorithm:
- For a set of top lags K (e.g., top 5), consider all pairs (i,j) with i+j=p and i<j (and i≠j). There are O(nK) pairs. For each pair, if a_i > a_j, move ε = 0.1 * (a_i - a_j) / 2? Actually to equalize, transfer t=(a_i-a_j)/2*λ. For small λ. Candidate = a; candidate[i]-=t; candidate[j]+=t; normalize (sum unchanged). Evaluate max. If improves, accept. Repeat scanning pairs. This is coordinate descent. Number of pairs K*n/2 ~5*8192=40k; evaluating conv for each pair impossible. But we can estimate or batch. We can update multiple pairs and evaluate batch line search.

Batch: Build a transfer vector δ = -t for sources, +t for destinations. For active peaks, choose t proportional to a_i - a_j (for each pair, transfer from larger to smaller by fraction λ). Then candidate = project(a + λ totalδ). Evaluate. If improves, accept. This is similar to gradient but specifically equalizes pairs. We can generate δ for each peak:
```
δ = np.zeros(n)
for p in top_lags:
  for i in valid:
    j=p-i
    if i<j:
       diff = a[i] - a[j]
       delta = λ * diff  # transfer half? To reduce product, add 0.5 λ diff to smaller, subtract?
       If diff>0: a_i decreases, a_j increases: δ[i]-=0.5*diff*λ; δ[j]+=0.5*diff*λ.
       If diff<0: opposite.
```
Equivalent δ_j sign. This vector has zero sum per peak. Candidate = a + η δ. This reduces pair products for those peaks (locally second-order). It may also improve. This is a useful polishing method!

Implement pair_balance step vectorized carefully:
```
def pair_balance_delta(a, lags, eta):
    n=a.size; d=np.zeros(n)
    for k in lags:
       lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if lo>hi: continue
       start=max(0,k-hi); end=min(n-1,k-lo)+1
       seg_i=a[lo:hi+1]
       seg_j=a[start:end][::-1]
       diff=seg_i - seg_j
       d[lo:hi+1] += -eta * diff
    return d
```
Does this overcount diagonal? diff zero. Sum d: For each k, sum over i diff_i =0? Since j permutation of i, sum a_i - a_j=0, so sum d 0. Good.
Maybe for multiple lags, sum preserved. Candidate = a + d. If negative, project to simplex (sum1, nonneg). Projection slightly alters sum. Good.
Line search: Use `scale = max(abs(d))` maybe; factors.

Will this reduce exact max? Since for top lag, c_k decreases to first order? Let's compute c_k change under δ = -η diff: c_k = ∑ a_i a_j. δ effect first order ∑ (δ_i a_j + a_i δ_j) = 2∑_i δ_i a_j for unordered? For pair, change =δ_i a_j + a_i δ_j = ηB(A-B) - ηA(A-B)= -η(A^2-B^2) negative if A>B. Good. So it reduces sum of ordered products? It reduces if variance positive. Perfect.

Let's write core functions now mentally.

```
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=rfft(a,size)
    c=irfft(A*A, size)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c[c<0]=0  # but this modifies
    return c
```
`c[c<0]=0` creates copy. Good.

`max_only(a)`:
```
return conv_self(a).max()
```

`normalize(a, n, S=1.0)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
a=np.clip(a,0,None)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-15: a=np.full(n, S/n)
else: a=a*(S/s)
return a
```
If input len !=n, maybe resize? For candidates, ensure.

`project_simplex(v,S)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
n=v.size
w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
cs=np.cumsum(w)
idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
th=(cs-S)/idx
valid=th <= w
if np.any(valid): rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]))+1; th=th[rho-1]
else: rho=n; th=(cs[-1]-S)/n
return np.maximum(v - th,0.0)
```
Could have v with nan? no.

`topk_weights(c,k,beta)`:
```
L=c.size; k=min(int(k),L)
if k<=0: return empty
if k==1:
   idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))],dtype=int); return idx, np.array([1.0])
part=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
vals=c[part]
if beta <= 0:
   e=np.ones(k)
else:
   vmax=vals.max()
   e=np.exp(beta*(vals-vmax))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: e=np.ones(k); s=k
   e/=s
return part,e
```
If beta extremely large, e may underflow for nonmax, only max 1. That's fine.

`grad_topk_direct(a, idx, vals)`:
Use local n.
```
g=np.zeros(n)
for kk,val in zip(idx,vals):
   if val==0: continue
   k=int(kk)
   lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
   if lo>hi: continue
   start=max(0,k-hi); end=min(n-1,k-lo)+1
   seg=a[start:end][::-1]
   g[lo:hi+1] += (2.0*val)*seg
return g
```
`pair_balance_direct(a, idx, vals)`:
```
d=np.zeros(n)
for kk,val:
  d[lo:hi+1] += val*(seg - seg_i)? We want seg_j - seg_i.
  seg_i=a[lo:hi+1]
  seg_j=a[start:end][::-1]
  d[lo:hi+1] += val*(seg_j - seg_i)
return d
```
Check sum: for each k, sum seg_i = sum seg_j, zero.

def smooth_score(c,beta):
  m=c.max(); if beta<1e-8: return m
  return (m + np.log(np.exp(beta*(c-m)).sum()))/beta
```

def add_perturbation(a, rng, scale):
   # smooth noise

Also try deterministic smooth noise: `noise = np.convolve(rng.normal(0,1,n), kernel, mode='same')`. We can implement `smooth_noise(n,rng,band=64,amp)` top-level.
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, amp, band=64):
    r=np.convolve(rng.normal(0,1,n), np.ones(band)/band, mode='same')
    r = r - r.mean()
    return amp*r/ (np.std(r)+1e-15)
```
Cost O(n) rare.

In no_improve, choose if no_improve>2000 larger amp 0.02*scale and reset best; else 0.005.

Need be careful: if no_improve increases but we don't update theta after perturb? Reset.

Let's refine `analytic_sequence`:
```
def analytic_sequence(n, alpha=0.5, cos_f=0, cos_amp=0.0):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5
    base = (x ** (-alpha)) * ((n - x) ** (-alpha))
    if cos_f > 0:
       base = base * (1.0 + cos_amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*float(cos_f)*np.arange(n)/float(n)))
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-300)
    return normalize(base, n)
```
If cos_amp negative could make negative; we clamp.

`random_smooth_sequence`:
```
x=np.arange(n)
v=np.zeros(n)
R=4
for r in range(R):
   freq = int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//64)))
   phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
   amp = rng.uniform(0.3,1.5)
   v += amp*np.sin(2π*freq*x/n + phase)
gamma = 0.8
a=np.exp(gamma*(v-v.mean()))
return normalize(a,n)
```
Potential under/overflow if gamma*range 4; okay.

`smooth_sequence`: Use Gaussian kernel length `2*width+1`.
```
def smooth_sequence(a, width):
   w=2*width+1
   sigma=max(1.0, width/3.0)
   xx=np.arange(w)-width
   k=np.exp(-xx*xx/(2*sigma*sigma)); k/=k.sum()
   s=np.convolve(a,k,mode='same')
   return normalize(s, len(a))
```
For width maybe 4,16.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
   old_n=len(a); if old_n==n_new return a.copy()
   old_x=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); new_x=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
   return normalize(b,n_new)
```
This maps endpoints to 1; maybe better use indices. Good.

`make_candidates(parent)`: top-level to avoid nested.
```
def make_candidates(parent, rng):
    cands=[]
    max_n=16384; min_n=1024
    if parent is not None and len>=32:
       p=normalize(parent, len(parent))
       cands.append(('parent', p))
       for w in (4,16): cands.append(('smooth'+w, smooth_sequence(p,w)))
       n0=len(p)
    else:
       n0=4096; p=analytic_sequence(n0,0.5); cands.append(('ana',p))
    n0=len(cands[0][1])
    n_list=[n0]
    if n0//2 >= 1024: n_list.append(n0//2)
    if n0*2 <= 16384: n_list.append(n0*2)
    # dedup: for each n, add resampled parent and analytic
    for n in n_list:
       if parent is not None:
          cands.append(('resamp%d'%n, resample_sequence(p,n)))
       for alpha in (0.45,0.5,0.55):
          cands.append(('ana%d_a%d'%(n,int(alpha*100)), analytic_sequence(n,alpha)))
    # random
    cands.append(('rand0', random_smooth_sequence(n0,rng,1.0,4)))
    cands.append(('rand1', random_smooth_sequence(n0,rng,1.5,6)))
    # sparse
    for frac in (0.6,0.8):
       m=int(n0*frac); idx=rng.choice(n0,m,replace=False); s=np.zeros(n0); s[idx]=1; cands.append(('sparse%.1f'%frac, normalize(s,n0)))
    # limit
    # score and sort
    scored=[]
    for name,a in cands:
       if len(a) < 16: continue
       a=normalize(a,len(a))
       cc=conv_self(a); obj=2*len(a)*float(cc.max())
       scored.append((obj,name,len(a),a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda t:t[0])   # lambda forbidden! Need avoid lambda. Use `scored.sort()` if tuple first obj. Yes tuple sort by first then name.
    return scored
```
No lambda. Good.

Let's write `run_projected_gradient` precisely:
```
def run_projected_gradient(a_init, budget, rng, name='', mode='balance', beta0=2.0, beta1=10.0, k0=1, k1=6, k_rate=1000, beta_rate=1.001, theta0=0.05, theta_max=0.5, momentum=0.1):
    n=len(a_init); start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0.0,budget)
    x=normalize(a_init,n,1.0)
    c=conv_self(x); cur=float(c.max()); best=x.copy(); best_c=cur
    vel=np.zeros(n); theta=theta0; beta=beta0; k=k0; no_improve=0; it=0
    factors=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125)
    modes = (mode,)
    if mode == 'grad': modes=('grad','balance')
    else: modes=('balance','grad')
    last_out=start
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if it % k_rate == 0 and k < k1: k += 1
       if beta < beta1: beta = min(beta1, beta*beta_rate)
       idx, vals = topk_weights(c, k, beta)
       scale_pos = x[x > 0]
       scale = float(np.max(scale_pos)) if scale_pos.size else 1.0/n
       if scale < 1e-15: scale = 1.0/n
       base_step = theta * scale
       accepted=False
       chosen_x=None; chosen_c=None; cur_it=cur
       for dir_mode in modes:
          if dir_mode == 'grad':
             g=grad_topk_direct(x, idx, vals)
             gm=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
             if gm <= 1e-300: continue
             direction = -g/gm
          else:
             b=pair_balance_direct(x, idx, vals)
             bm=float(np.max(np.abs(b)))
             if bm <= 1e-300: continue
             direction = b/bm
          for fac in factors:
             step = base_step * fac
             if step < 1e-16: break
             cand = project_simplex(x + momentum*vel + step*direction, 1.0)
             ct=conv_self(cand); cur_t=float(ct.max())
             if cur_t < cur - 1e-18:
                chosen_x=cand; chosen_c=ct; cur_it=cur_t; accepted=True; break
          if accepted: break
       if accepted:
          vel = chosen_x - x
          x = chosen_x; c = chosen_c; cur = cur_it
          if cur < best_c - 1e-18:
             best_c=cur; best=x.copy(); no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve=0
          theta = min(theta_max, theta*(1.0 + 0.02*fac))  # fac from last inner? Need fac accessible. We'll set accepted_fac.
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          vel = np.zeros(n)
          theta = max(1e-5, theta*0.5)
          if no_improve > 800:
             if no_improve % 1000 ==0: print progress
             amp = 0.003*scale if no_improve < 4000 else 0.02*scale
             noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, amp)
             x = project_simplex(best + noise, 1.0)
             c=conv_self(x); cur=float(c.max())
             if cur < best_c:
                best_c=cur; best=x.copy()
             vel=np.zeros(n); theta=theta0; no_improve=0
       if it % 1000 ==0 or (time.monotonic()-last_out>10.0):
           print(name, 'it', it, 'obj=%.6f'% (2*n*best_c), 'cur=%.6f'%cur, 'theta=%.4g'%theta)
           last_out=time.monotonic()
    return best, best_c
```
Need `accepted_fac` variable defined before factor loops. We'll set `accepted_fac=fac` on accept.
If accepted after secondary mode, `fac` has value. Good.

`smooth_noise` top-level:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, amp):
    if amp <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    band = max(16, n//64)
    kernel = np.ones(band)/band
    r = np.convolve(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n), kernel, mode='same')
    sd = float(np.std(r))
    if sd < 1e-15: return np.zeros(n)
    return amp * (r - r.mean()) / sd
```

Potential issue: In `run_projected_gradient`, if `modes` tuple with same? Not.

What about if `dir_mode` grad and beta weights for top1; g maybe 0 if top lag at edge and all mass at same? Then try balance. If both zero, direction none; accepted false; no_improve increases.

Now, we need ensure `project_simplex` S = 1.0. It subtracts theta, may produce sum 1. For v with negative huge, theta maybe? OK.

```

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2 for nonnegative sequences.

Strategy:
- Keep the sequence normalized to sum 1, so objective = 2*n*max(conv).
- Use a projected subgradient / pair-balance local search with line search.
- The directional gradient is computed exactly for a small set of active
  (peak) convolution lags.
- Two complementary directions are used:
  1. negative subgradient of a smoothed top-k peak objective
  2. a pair-balancing direction that transfers mass between coordinates
     whose sums land on the active lags, reducing the quadratic peak locally.
- Several independent starting points are explored (parent, smoothed parent,
  analytic endpoint-singular shapes, random smooth shapes, resampled lengths),
  then the best result is polished for the remaining time budget.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft


def next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m (m >= 1)."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize(a, n, S=1.0):
    """Clip to nonnegative and rescale to sum S at length n."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        a = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return (a * (S / s)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cs - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
        theta = theta[rho - 1]
    else:
        rho = n
        theta = (cs[-1] - S) / float(n)
    out = v - theta
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def conv_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution of nonnegative a via FFT."""
    n = int(a.size)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(need)
    A = rfft(a, size)
    c = irfft(A * A, size)
    c = c[:need]
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def score_maxc(a):
    """Return max convolution coefficient for sum-normalized sequence."""
    return float(conv_self(a).max())


def score_objective(a):
    """Return 2*n*max(conv) for sum-normalized sequence."""
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * float(conv_self(a).max())


def topk_weights(c, k, beta):
    """
    Return indices and nonnegative normalized weights on the top-k c-values.
    beta=0 gives uniform weights on the top-k.
    """
    L = len(c)
    k = int(min(max(1, k), L))
    if k == 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        vals = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, vals

    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    vals_c = c[idx]
    if beta <= 0.0:
        vals = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64)
        vals /= float(k)
    else:
        vmx = float(vals_c.max())
        e = np.exp(beta * (vals_c - vmx))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            e = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(k)
        vals = e / s
    return idx.astype(int, copy=False), vals


def grad_topk_direct(a, idx, vals):
    """
    Exact gradient of sum_k vals[k] * c_k, where c = a*a (linear convolution).
    Uses only the supplied active lags.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk, val in zip(idx, vals):
        if val == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        start = max(0, k - hi)
        end = min(n - 1, k - lo) + 1
        seg = a[start:end][::-1]
        g[lo:hi + 1] += (2.0 * val) * seg
    return g


def pair_balance_direct(a, idx, vals):
    """
    Direction that locally reduces active peak coefficients by balancing
    pairs (i,j) with i+j=k:
        d_i = sum_k vals[k] * (a_{k-i} - a_i) for valid pairs.
    This is a second-order improving direction for the active lags.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk, val in zip(idx, vals):
        if val == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        start = max(0, k - hi)
        end = min(n - 1, k - lo) + 1
        seg_i = a[lo:hi + 1]
        seg_j = a[start:end][::-1]
        d[lo:hi + 1] += val * (seg_j - seg_i)
    return d


def smooth_sequence(a, width):
    """Circular-ish moving-average smoothing with small boundaries."""
    width = max(1, int(width))
    w = 2 * width + 1
    sigma = max(1.0, width / 3.0)
    xx = np.arange(w, dtype=np.float64) - width
    k = np.exp(-(xx * xx) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma))
    k /= k.sum()
    b = np.convolve(a, k, mode="same")
    return normalize(b, len(a), 1.0)


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Resample a sequence to a new length by linear interpolation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_new))
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize(b, int(n_new), 1.0)


def analytic_sequence(n, alpha=0.5, cos_f=0, cos_amp=0.0):
    """
    Endpoint-singular analytic initial shape.
    x^{-alpha} (n-x)^{-alpha}, optionally multiplied by a low-frequency
    cosine modulation.
    """
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    base = (x ** (-alpha)) * ((n - x) ** (-alpha))
    if cos_f > 0:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        base = base * (1.0 + float(cos_amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(cos_f) * t))
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-300)
    return normalize(base, n, 1.0)


def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng, gamma=1.0, terms=4):
    """Random low-frequency smooth positive sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxf = max(2, n // 64)
    for _ in range(int(terms)):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, maxf + 1))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.6))
        v += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * float(f) * x / float(n) + ph)
    v -= v.mean()
    a = np.exp(float(gamma) * v)
    return normalize(a, n, 1.0)


def sparse_sequence(n, rng, frac):
    """Equal positive weights on a random subset."""
    n = int(n)
    m = int(max(8, int(round(n * float(frac)))))
    m = min(m, n)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[idx] = 1.0
    return normalize(a, n, 1.0)


def smooth_noise(n, rng, amp):
    """Smooth random perturbation vector with standard deviation almost amp."""
    n = int(n)
    if amp <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    band = max(16, n // 64)
    raw = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    raw = np.convolve(raw, np.ones(band) / float(band), mode="same")
    s = float(np.std(raw))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    raw = raw - raw.mean()
    return float(amp) * raw / s


def make_candidates(parent, rng):
    """Create and quick-score a set of starting sequences."""
    cands = []

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 16 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.sum() > 1e-12:
                p = normalize(p, p.size, 1.0)
            else:
                p = None
    else:
        p = None

    if p is None:
        n0 = 4096
        p0 = analytic_sequence(n0, 0.5)
        cands.append(("base-analytic-0", p0))
    else:
        n0 = len(p)
        p0 = p
        cands.append(("parent-0", p0))
        cands.append(("parent-smooth-4", smooth_sequence(p0, 4)))
        cands.append(("parent-smooth-16", smooth_sequence(p0, 16)))

    n_list = [n0]
    if n0 // 2 >= 1024:
        n_list.append(n0 // 2)
    if n0 * 2 <= 16384:
        n_list.append(n0 * 2)

    for n in n_list:
        cands.append(("resamp-%d" % int(n), resample_sequence(p0, n)))
        for alpha in (0.45, 0.5, 0.55):
            cands.append(("ana-%d-a%d" % (int(n), int(round(alpha * 100.0))),
                          analytic_sequence(n, alpha)))
        cands.append(("ana-itab-%d" % int(n),
                      analytic_sequence(n, 0.5, cos_f=1, cos_amp=0.15)))
        cands.append(("ana-ibase-%d" % int(n),
                      analytic_sequence(n, 0.5, cos_f=3, cos_amp=-0.10)))

    cands.append(("rand-smooth-0", random_smooth_sequence(n0, rng, 1.0, 4)))
    cands.append(("rand-smooth-1", random_smooth_sequence(n0, rng, 1.6, 6)))
    cands.append(("rand-smooth-2", random_smooth_sequence(n0, rng, 0.9, 8)))
    cands.append(("sparse-0.60", sparse_sequence(n0, rng, 0.60)))
    cands.append(("sparse-0.80", sparse_sequence(n0, rng, 0.80)))

    scored = []
    for idx, (name, a) in enumerate(cands):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size < 16 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            continue
        a = normalize(a, a.size, 1.0)
        obj = score_objective(a)
        if np.isfinite(obj):
            scored.append((obj, idx, name, a))

    scored.sort()
    return scored


def run_projected_gradient(
        a_init, budget, rng,
        name="",
        mode="balance",
        beta0=2.0,
        beta1=10.0,
        k0=1,
        k1=8,
        beta_rate=1.001,
        theta0=0.05,
        theta_max=0.5,
        momentum=0.1
):
    """
    Projected local search using exact active-lag subgradients and
    pair-balancing directions. Returns (best_sequence, best_maxc).
    """
    n = int(a_init.size)
    x = normalize(a_init, n, 1.0)
    c = conv_self(x)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best = x.copy()
    best_c = cur

    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = float(theta0)
    beta = float(beta0)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget))
    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125)

    if mode == "grad":
        modes = ("grad", "balance")
    else:
        modes = ("balance", "grad")

    last_print = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if beta < beta1:
            beta = min(float(beta1), beta * float(beta_rate))
        k = int(k0)
        k = min(int(k1), max(int(k0), int(beta / 2.0) + 1))

        idx, vals = topk_weights(c, k, beta)

        pos = x[x > 0.0]
        scale = float(np.max(pos)) if pos.size else (1.0 / float(n))
        if scale <= 1e-300:
            scale = 1.0 / float(n)

        base_step = theta * scale

        accepted = False
        chosen_x = None
        chosen_c = None
        chosen_cur = cur
        accepted_fac = 1.0

        for dir_mode in modes:
            if dir_mode == "grad":
                g = grad_topk_direct(x, idx, vals)
                gm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
                if gm <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                direction = -g / gm
            else:
                d = pair_balance_direct(x, idx, vals)
                dm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
                if dm <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                direction = d / dm

            for fac in factors:
                step = base_step * float(fac)
                if step <= 1e-16:
                    break
                cand = project_simplex(x + momentum * vel + step * direction, 1.0)
                ct = conv_self(cand)
                cur_t = float(ct.max())
                if cur_t < cur - 1e-18:
                    accepted = True
                    chosen_x = cand
                    chosen_c = ct
                    chosen_cur = cur_t
                    accepted_fac = float(fac)
                    break
            if accepted:
                break

        if accepted:
            vel = chosen_x - x
            x = chosen_x
            c = chosen_c
            cur = chosen_cur
            if cur < best_c - 1e-18:
                best_c = cur
                best = x.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            theta = min(float(theta_max), theta * (1.0 + 0.02 * accepted_fac))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            theta = max(1e-5, theta * 0.5)

            if no_improve > 800:
                amp = 0.004 * scale if no_improve < 4000 else 0.025 * scale
                noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, amp)
                x = project_simplex(best + noise, 1.0)
                c = conv_self(x)
                cur = float(c.max())
                if cur < best_c:
                    best_c = cur
                    best = x.copy()
                vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                theta = float(theta0)
                no_improve = 1

        if it % 1000 == 0 or (time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0):
            print("%s it=%d obj=%.6f cur=%.6f k=%d beta=%.2f theta=%.4g" % (
                name, it, 2.0 * n * best_c, cur, k, beta, theta))
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    best = normalize(best, n, 1.0)
    return best, float(best_c)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2385):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
    evaluate_sequence. The function returns the best list found within
    the provided time budget.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    overall_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 16 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    scored = make_candidates(parent, rng)
    if not scored:
        fallback = [0.5] * 256
        try:
            v = evaluate_sequence(fallback)
        except Exception:
            v = np.nan
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            fallback = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4]
        return fallback

    scored = scored[:8]
    global_obj = float(scored[0][0])
    global_a = np.asarray(scored[0][3], dtype=np.float64).copy()
    global_n = int(global_a.size)
    print("initial best obj=%.6f name=%s n=%d" % (global_obj, scored[0][2], global_n))

    # ---------------- exploration ----------------
    remaining = overall_deadline - time.monotonic()
    explor_fraction = 0.45
    explor_time = explor_fraction * remaining

    if explor_time > 60.0:
        top = scored[:6]
        weights = (1.0, 0.75, 0.55, 0.40, 0.30, 0.25)
        raw = []
        for i, item in enumerate(top):
            n_item = int(len(item[3]))
            cost = math.sqrt(max(1.0, float(n_item) / 8192.0))
            w = weights[i] if i < len(weights) else 0.2
            raw.append(w / cost)
        sraw = float(sum(raw)) or 1.0

        for i, item in enumerate(top):
            time_left = overall_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if time_left < 10.0:
                break
            obj0, _, name, a0 = item
            n_item = int(a0.size)

            budget = min(explor_time * (raw[i] / sraw), time_left)
            if budget < 8.0:
                continue

            if i == 0:
                a_new, c_new = run_projected_gradient(
                    a0, budget, rng,
                    name="expl0-" + name,
                    mode="balance",
                    beta0=2.0,
                    beta1=8.0,
                    k0=1,
                    k1=6,
                    beta_rate=1.001,
                    theta0=0.08,
                    theta_max=0.4,
                    momentum=0.1
                )
            elif i % 2 == 0:
                a_new, c_new = run_projected_gradient(
                    a0, budget, rng,
                    name="expl%d-%s" % (i, name),
                    mode="balance",
                    beta0=4.0,
                    beta1=30.0,
                    k0=2,
                    k1=16,
                    beta_rate=1.002,
                    theta0=0.20,
                    theta_max=1.0,
                    momentum=0.3
                )
            else:
                a_new, c_new = run_projected_gradient(
                    a0, budget, rng,
                    name="expl%d-%s" % (i, name),
                    mode="grad",
                    beta0=5.0,
                    beta1=35.0,
                    k0=2,
                    k1=18,
                    beta_rate=1.002,
                    theta0=0.20,
                    theta_max=1.0,
                    momentum=0.3
                )

            a_new = normalize(a_new, int(a_new.size), 1.0)
            obj_new = 2.0 * float(a_new.size) * float(c_new)
            if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < global_obj - 1e-12:
                global_obj = obj_new
                global_a = a_new.copy()
                global_n = int(a_new.size)
                print("exploration improved obj=%.6f name=%s n=%d" % (obj_new, name, global_n))

    # ---------------- final polishing ----------------
    remaining = overall_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 25.0:
        b1 = 0.60 * remaining
        a_new, c_new = run_projected_gradient(
            global_a, b1, rng,
            name="final-balance-fine",
            mode="balance",
            beta0=1.0,
            beta1=5.0,
            k0=1,
            k1=4,
            beta_rate=1.0004,
            theta0=0.04,
            theta_max=0.25,
            momentum=0.1
        )
        a_new = normalize(a_new, int(a_new.size), 1.0)
        obj_new = 2.0 * float(a_new.size) * float(c_new)
        if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < global_obj - 1e-12:
            global_obj = obj_new
            global_a = a_new.copy()
            global_n = int(a_new.size)
            print("final-balance improved obj=%.6f n=%d" % (obj_new, global_n))

    remaining = overall_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 20.0:
        b2 = 0.70 * remaining
        a_new, c_new = run_projected_gradient(
            global_a, b2, rng,
            name="final-gradient-fine",
            mode="grad",
            beta0=1.0,
            beta1=8.0,
            k0=1,
            k1=6,
            beta_rate=1.0005,
            theta0=0.05,
            theta_max=0.30,
            momentum=0.15
        )
        a_new = normalize(a_new, int(a_new.size), 1.0)
        obj_new = 2.0 * float(a_new.size) * float(c_new)
        if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < global_obj - 1e-12:
            global_obj = obj_new
            global_a = a_new.copy()
            global_n = int(a_new.size)
            print("final-gradient improved obj=%.6f n=%d" % (obj_new, global_n))

    remaining = overall_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 15.0:
        b3 = 0.80 * remaining
        a_new, c_new = run_projected_gradient(
            global_a, b3, rng,
            name="final-ultra-fine",
            mode="balance",
            beta0=1.0,
            beta1=3.0,
            k0=1,
            k1=3,
            beta_rate=1.0002,
            theta0=0.025,
            theta_max=0.15,
            momentum=0.08
        )
        a_new = normalize(a_new, int(a_new.size), 1.0)
        obj_new = 2.0 * float(a_new.size) * float(c_new)
        if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < global_obj - 1e-12:
            global_obj = obj_new
            global_a = a_new.copy()
            global_n = int(a_new.size)
            print("final-ultra improved obj=%.6f n=%d" % (obj_new, global_n))

    # ---------------- finalize and verify ----------------
    final_a = normalize(global_a, int(global_a.size), 1.0)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]
    final_obj = score_objective(final_a)
    print("FINAL internal: length=%d obj=%.6f maxc=%.10f time=%.1fs" % (
        len(final_list), final_obj, final_a.__len__() and score_maxc(final_a),
        time.monotonic() - t0))

    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        ver = np.nan

    if np.isfinite(ver):
        print("Verified objective: %.6f" % ver)
    else:
        print("Verification failed; using fallback.")
        final_list = [0.5] * 256
        try:
            ver2 = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
            print("Fallback verified: %.6f" % ver2)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return final_list
```
